Load for BP00 step 3, for any packet adding a rung-4 scenario, and for anything touching Tools/run-gauntlet.ps1. The contract (docs/contracts/testing.md) owns the ladder and is LAW; this skill is HOW rung 4 gets built. ([JDMAXilius/Multi-agents-class](https://github.com/JDMAXilius/Multi-agents-class/tree/HEAD/breachpoint/.claude/skills/gauntlet-testing))
- **gauntlet-verify**: Loads only when the user explicitly asks to verify the gauntlet, asks whether a gauntlet run is actually done, or when a gauntlet run has reached a stopped or converged state with no consensus on disk. Spawns independent quality and integrity verifiers that never saw the build, collects their verdicts to disk, and computes consensus by script. Do not load for ordinary tasks, quick edits, single-shot drafts, routine reviews, or any request that does not name the gauntlet. ([Israelmusondaayliffe/plugins](https://github.com/Israelmusondaayliffe/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/gauntlet/skills/gauntlet-verify) · ★2)
- **gauntlet-verify**: Use when the user explicitly invokes $gauntlet-loop:gauntlet-verify to evaluate a project with fresh independent judges, acceptance-to-evidence traceability, adversarial checks, and a bounded final verdict. Never use implicitly. ([Israelmusondaayliffe/plugins](https://github.com/Israelmusondaayliffe/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/gauntlet-loop/skills/gauntlet-verify) · ★2)
- **gauntlet-workflow**: Runs a gauntlet loop as a Workflow script instead of a pasted prompt - a deterministic outer loop that builds each piece, grades it with a fresh harsh critic against a concrete bar, feeds the critic's single biggest gap back to the builder, and keeps going until every piece passes. Use when the goal has a real inspectable bar and you want the rounds enforced by code rather than by an agent's good intentions. Triggers on "/gauntlet-workflow", "gauntlet workflow", "gauntlet this as a workflow", "loop until it beats X, deterministically". ([jonnyasmith/skills](https://github.com/jonnyasmith/skills/tree/HEAD/archive/gauntlet-workflow))
- **gauntlet-workflow**: Runs a gauntlet loop as a workflowz eval cell instead of a pasted prompt - a deterministic outer loop that builds each piece, grades it with a fresh harsh critic against a concrete bar, feeds the critic's single biggest gap back to the builder, and keeps going until every piece passes. Use when the goal has a real inspectable bar and you want the rounds enforced by code rather than by an agent's good intentions. Triggers on "/gauntlet-workflow", "gauntlet workflow", "gauntlet this as a workflow", "loop until it beats X, deterministically". ([jonnyasmith/skills](https://github.com/jonnyasmith/skills/tree/HEAD/.omp/skills/gauntlet-workflow))
- **gauntlex:compare**: Diff two GAUNTLEX Resilience Reports — shows ARS delta, attack category shifts, new CWE coverage, and whether the codebase is improving or regressing. Returns exit 1 if ARS regression &gt; 0.05. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/compare) · ★1)
- **gauntlex:doctor**: Full GAUNTLEX environment health check — model reachable, ChromaDB writable, AVF fixtures pass, no unexpected outbound network (air-gap verification). Use before first run or when debugging failures. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/doctor) · ★1)
- **gauntlex:learn**: Ingest a completed GAUNTLEX run into the Knowledge Forge and update the Adaptive Brain's attack effectiveness scores. Call after reviewing a completed run to reinforce learning. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/learn) · ★1)
- **gauntlex:report**: Render a stored GAUNTLEX Resilience Report as Markdown, HTML, or structured JSON. Can attach to a GitHub PR comment. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/report) · ★1)
- **gauntlex:run**: Run a full GAUNTLEX adversarial session on a spec file or GitHub issue URL. Invokes the Gauntlex (Builder + Breaker concurrent) and outputs an Adversarial Resilience Score (ARS) with a Resilience Report. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/run) · ★1)
- **gauntlex:validate**: Dry-run GAUNTLEX validation — parses the spec, resolves policy playbooks, checks model availability, and runs the AVF golden fixture gate. No attacks are fired. Exit 1 if any check fails. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/validate) · ★1)
- **gauntlex:verify**: Re-derive the SHA-256 integrity hash over a Resilience Report's attack array and confirm it matches the stored hash. Detects tampered reports. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/verify) · ★1)
- **gaurava-store-screenshots**: Produce Gaurava's App Store screenshots in the Retro Rubberhose Mascot style (cream/mustard/pink/mint, Cooper-Black headlines with a coral squiggle word, white-gloved can mascot, paper grain, REAL iPhone frame). Seeds a believable 5-month GLP-1 journey into the simulator, captures the real app AND the ecosystem surfaces (widgets, Lock Screen, Control Center, Live Activity, Watch), then renders 1320x2868 review decks. Use when asked for App Store / marketing screenshots, store listing art, or a marketing deck for Gaurava. Wraps the base `app-store-screenshots` skill. - **gaussian-mixture-clustering-of-surface-protein-markers**: Use when CITE-seq data provides surface-protein ADT levels for markers (e.g., CD16/CD56) whose RNA is dropout-obscured, and you need protein-derived sub-population labels to quantify or benchmark RNA-based (or denoised) recovery of that structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-clustering-of-surface-protein-markers))
- **gaussian-mixture-denoising-of-adt-data**: Use when normalizing droplet-based protein (ADT) data from CITE-seq, ASAP-seq, or TEA-seq where ambient antibody noise and cell-to-cell technical variation confound protein values and raw/CLR values track empty-droplet signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-denoising-of-adt-data))
- **gaussian-mixture-density-decomposition**: Use when a population of per-cell confidence scores (e.g., Wilcoxon rank-sum scores on marker genes) must be split into validated vs. unvalidated sub-populations without an arbitrary hard cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gaussian-mixture-density-decomposition))
- **gaussian-mixture-em-decoupling**: Use when analyzing scCRISPR-seq cell-by-gene matrices where perturbation (Y) and control (Z) groups have differing cluster proportions, so naive fold-change estimation is distorted by infected proportion bias and the true cellular response must be decoupled. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-em-decoupling))
- **gaussian-mixture-em-fitting**: Use when quantifying extranuclear RNA contamination per droplet in droplet-based snRNA-seq: the percent of reads spliced is bimodal with nuclear and background droplets straddling ~50%, and a sample-specific midpoint separating the two distributions must be estimated per experiment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-em-fitting))
- **gaussian-mixture-expectation-maximization-estimation**: Use when performing differential abundance analysis of compositional microbiome data where differences in sampling fractions across samples introduce bias that must be estimated from the data itself. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/gaussian-mixture-expectation-maximization-estimation))
- **gaussian-mixture-fitting-of-hashtag-distributions**: Use when demultiplexing cell hashing experiments and per-hashtag cutoffs must distinguish true positive from background signal, especially with many hashtags or imbalanced sample sizes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-fitting-of-hashtag-distributions))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-clustering-of-gene-expression-pcs**: Use when expert-provided scribble annotations are unavailable and you need an initial non-spatial domain labeling of Visium or ST spots from principal components of raw gene expression as input to automated scribble generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-clustering-of-gene-expression-pcs))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-clustering-of-single-cell-transcriptomes**: Use when you need to partition unsynchronized single-cell transcriptome data (e.g. log2(TPM+1) cycle-base-gene expression) into a user-defined number of clusters k before reconstructing a pseudo time-series. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-clustering-of-single-cell-transcriptomes))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-em-estimation**: Use when scCRISPR-seq perturbation vs control groups differ in cluster proportions (infected proportion bias) and you need per-gRNA cellular responses decoupled from that bias on selected infection-biased PCs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-em-estimation))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-estimation**: Use when fitting a calibration curve from calibration-standard injections (per-feature peak_area from a FeatureDB/mzML report) for targeted LC-MS/MS quantitation where a generic linear fit is insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-estimation))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-fitting**: Use when a user-supplied CSV gene-expression data frame (gene identifiers plus log-fold-change values) must be partitioned into distinct expression-pattern clusters using unsupervised, probability-based Gaussian mixture modelling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-fitting--6d928929dbd1e6ca))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-fitting**: Use when assigning gRNAs to cells in a single-cell CRISPR screen and per-guide UMI count distributions must be split into endogenous vs ambient components without a user-defined fixed UMI threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-fitting--f2ab6b3b35254e00))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-fitting-per-cell**: Use when normalizing CITE-seq/ASAP-seq/TEA-seq ADT data with dsb step II, or when validating that k=2 Gaussian mixtures fit per-cell protein distributions via BIC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-fitting-per-cell))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-initialization**: Use when fitting a LUCID model on exposures G, omics Z (possibly with sporadic or list-wise missing values), and outcome Y, and you need deterministic starting values for the EM algorithm over K latent clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-initialization))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-subclone-separability**: Use when comparing how accurately different cell-cycle correction methods (Cyclum, Seurat, ccRemover, PC1–5) preserve or recover subclone structure in scRNA-seq data containing two known subpopulations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-subclone-separability))
- **gaussian-mixture-models**: Comprehensive guide to gaussian mixture models. Master the concepts, implementation, best practices, and real-world applications of gaussian mixture models in professional environments. ([Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy](https://github.com/Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/gaussian-mixture-models) · ★1)
- **gaussian-mixture-posterior-thresholding**: Use when continuous numeric trait columns (e.g., yogurt metabolite concentrations) must be converted to binary case/control labels for Scoary2 association testing and uncertain isolates need a principled exclusion rule. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gaussian-mixture-posterior-thresholding))
- **gaussian-mixture-prior-initialization**: Use when implementing or reproducing SCALE's VAE+GMM architecture on scATAC-seq data (e.g. Forebrain.h5ad peak-by-barcode matrix) and a tight posterior estimate over latent features is needed for clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-prior-initialization))
- **gaussian-mixture-rejection-threshold-fitting**: Use when binarized scRNA-seq marker counts per cell type show a bimodal distribution and a defensible cutoff is needed to label clusters as 'unassigned' (unknown) rather than force an annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-rejection-threshold-fitting))
- **gaussian-mixture-reliability-modeling**: Use when transferring labels from annotated scRNA-seq to unlabeled scATAC-seq and you must decide per-cell which target cells can safely be merged into the annotated source during iterative integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-reliability-modeling))
- **gaussian-mixture-simulation-on-unit-circle**: Use when benchmarking clustering algorithms on non-convex synthetic shapes (rings, half-moons) where ground-truth labels are available and purity scores against GMM, K-means, DBSCAN, OPTICS, or spectral clustering baselines are required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-simulation-on-unit-circle))
- **gaussian-mixture-technical-component-denoising**: Use when ADT/protein counts have already been ambient-corrected against empty droplets and residual cell-to-cell technical noise (correlated with isotype controls and background mean) must be regressed out before clustering or UMAP. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-technical-component-denoising))
- **gaussian-mixture-umi-threshold-fitting**: Use when assigning gRNAs to cells in single-cell CRISPR screens whose per-gRNA UMI distributions vary widely (low vs high UMI experiments), where a fixed min_umi threshold over- or under-assigns cells; triggered by UMI-histogram bimodality or inter-experiment differences in average gRNA UMIs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-umi-threshold-fitting))

Use for Gaussian FCH molecular dimensions and box visualization. ([Nanhuai-X/gaussian-molecule-workflow](https://github.com/Nanhuai-X/gaussian-molecule-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/gaussian-molecular-box) · ★12)

Use for database structure retrieval with mandatory human structure confirmation, charge and multiplicity resolution, Gaussian/formchk execution, ESP surface extrema, HOMO/LUMO energies and cubes, ELF 2D/3D maps, molecular-box dimensions, thermochemistry, and optimized-structure export. The workflow requires preflight for Gaussian, formchk, Multiwfn, VMD, Python, PyYAML, NumPy, Pillow, and Matplotlib. - **gaussian-noise-addition-to-fits-images**: Use when simulating an astronomical image that needs realistic pixel-level noise after drawing a galaxy convolved with a PSF, e.g. reproducing demo1-style GalSim outputs with a fixed random seed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/gaussian-noise-addition-to-fits-images))
- **gaussian-noise-injection-at-multiple-sigma-levels**: Use when benchmarking the noise robustness of computed isotopologue profiles (e.g., single-tracer 13C vs multi-tracer 13C/18O glucose moiety models) and you need simulated replicate datasets at several error magnitudes before renormalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-noise-injection-at-multiple-sigma-levels))
- **gaussian-noise-injection-for-robustness-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating how a multi-omics integration method degrades on intentionally corrupted inputs — e.g. comparing SSGATE vs baselines on noisy BMNC/SLN111_D1 anndata pairs under a paper's robustness protocol. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gaussian-noise-injection-for-robustness-benchmarking))
- **gaussian-noise-perturbation-of-scrnaseq-data**: Use when you need to quantify the noise robustness of a single-cell trajectory inference or alignment pipeline by sweeping Gaussian noise levels on synthetic scRNA-seq data and recording performance-metric distributions per noise level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-noise-perturbation-of-scrnaseq-data))
- **gaussian-noise-robustness-testing**: Use when a paired microbiome-metabolome model has been trained in two configurations (full vs. annotated-subset outputs) and you must quantify whether multivariate learning confers robustness to input perturbation before comparing per-metabolite SCCs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-noise-robustness-testing))
- **gaussian-particle-designer**: 3D Gaussian volumetric rendering specialist. Debugs anisotropic stretching, transparency artifacts, cube-like rendering, and particle visual quality. Uses gaussian-analyzer MCP tools for particle structure validation and shader optimization. ([IvanMazeppa/PlasmaDXR](https://github.com/IvanMazeppa/PlasmaDXR/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gaussian-particle-designer))
- **gaussian-peak-deconvolution**: Use when an ion chromatogram's targeted compound peak is coeluted with other peaks (e.g., isomers sharing a transition with close RTs) and per-peak abundances must be separated rather than summed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-peak-deconvolution))
- **gaussian-peak-modeling**: Use when a 1D MS signal (e.g., XIC or ATD extracted from an MZA/HDF5 file via mzapy) contains overlapping or noise-affected peaks that need Gaussian model fitting, peak-parameter estimation, or SNR-based peak quality assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-peak-modeling))
- **gaussian-peak-shape-evaluation**: Use when after peak detection on a composite mass track has identified candidate peaks in a mass chromatogram, and before compiling the final feature table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-peak-shape-evaluation) · 4 copies)
- **gaussian-peak-shape-fitting**: Use when comparing feature counts from LC-MS processing tools (asari, XCMS, MZmine) and you need an objective peak-quality metric to determine whether extra features are genuinely better coverage or low-quality peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-peak-shape-fitting))
- **gaussian-peak-shape-validation**: Use when performing untargeted GC × GC–MS peak picking from raw CDF files and false-positive blobs (incomplete peak shapes, sub-threshold S/N) must be discarded before deconvolution by PARAFAC/PARAFAC2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-peak-shape-validation))
- **gaussian-peakshape-fitting-evaluation**: Use when after peak detection on mass track segments using find_peaks, when you need to distinguish genuine chromatographic peaks from noise-induced false positives or irregular shapes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-peakshape-fitting-evaluation) · 2 copies)
- **gaussian-prior-chi-squared-likelihood-evaluation**: Use when a posterior must be defined numerically before MCMC sampling in CBL: a chi-squared likelihood from model-data comparison plus Gaussian priors on cosmological parameters, to be explored by Metropolis-Hastings or stretch-move samplers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/gaussian-prior-chi-squared-likelihood-evaluation))
- **gaussian-prior-specification-cosmological-parameters**: Use when running an MCMC posterior analysis of Big Bang Nucleosynthesis abundances where neutron lifetime and baryon density must be Gaussian-constrained and nuclear-rate uncertainties propagated log-normally. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/gaussian-prior-specification-cosmological-parameters))
- **gaussian-process-kernel-structure-learning**: Use when a time series (e.g., astronomical light curves of stellar variability or exoplanet transits) must be modelled with a Gaussian Process but no covariance function can be justified a priori, and accurate uncertainty estimation is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/gaussian-process-kernel-structure-learning))
- **gaussian-process-length-scale-estimation**: Use when spot-level spatial transcriptomics data (e.g., Visium DLPFC counts plus coordinates) must be screened for SVGs whose spatial expression ranges differ, or when per-gene length scale parameters are the analysis target. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-process-length-scale-estimation))
- **gaussian-process-materials**: This skill covers Gaussian process regression (GPR) for materials and chemistry datasets: descriptor selection, feature scaling, kernel design, small-data modeling, calibrated uncertainty, heteroscedastic noise, cross-validation, multi-output and multi-fidelity extensions, and integration with Bayesian optimization and active learning. Invoke this skill when building a data-efficient surrogate mod ([SFETNI/Deep-Matter-Chem-Skills](https://github.com/SFETNI/Deep-Matter-Chem-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/surrogate-active-learning/gaussian-process-materials) · ★11)
- **gaussian-process-meta-learning-prior-optimization**: Use when you must project DNN-predicted retention times to a specific chromatographic method using only ~10 identified molecules with predicted-experimental RT pairs, while also getting credible intervals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-process-meta-learning-prior-optimization))
- **gaussian-process-mlp-hybrid**: Discussion on Gaussian Process and MLP hybrid models for uncertainty estimation. Use when exploring machine learning model architectures, uncertainty quantification, or ensemble methods for drug discovery and similar applications. ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/tree/HEAD/data/gaussian-process-mlp-hybrid) · ★21 · 7 copies)
- **gaussian-process-noise-modelling**: Use when fitting RV timeseries (e.g. Barnard's star/GJ 699, HARPS-type instruments) where stellar activity or correlated noise may mimic planetary signals and competing noise models (jitter, moving-average, GP) must be compared by Evidence (ln Z). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/gaussian-process-noise-modelling))
- **gaussian-process-regression-3d-expression-modeling**: Use when consecutive ST slices have been 3D-aligned by Scube (e.g., the STARmap mouse brain dataset) and you need to model continuous gene-expression surfaces in 3D and identify spatially varying genes via Bayes Factor scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-process-regression-3d-expression-modeling))
- **gaussian-process-regression-model-fitting**: Use when after you have accumulated experimental MS data from ≥2 LC gradient trials, extracted separation efficiency metrics (retention time spacing) from each trial, and encoded each gradient as a feature vector. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-process-regression-model-fitting) · 2 copies)
- **gaussian-process-stellar-activity-modelling**: Use when 38-point-class HARPS RV time series show correlated stellar activity noise that a plain Keplerian fit cannot absorb, and joint modelling with TESS sectors plus ground-based transits (PEST, TRAPPIST-South) is needed to separate planetary signal from activity jitter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/gaussian-process-stellar-activity-modelling))
- **gaussian-process-surrogate-regression**: Use when a sequential optimization loop needs a probabilistic surrogate fitted to small experimental data (e.g., LC gradient parameters vs. separation efficiency) to support acquisition-function-based selection of the next experiment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-process-surrogate-regression))
- **gaussian-process-uncertainty-quantification**: Use when after collecting observed separation efficiency scores at sampled gradient conditions and you need to propose the next gradient to evaluate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-process-uncertainty-quantification) · 2 copies)

- **gaussian-rbf-kernel-regression**: Use when noisy per-cell scVelo RNA velocity must be aggregated to a lower-resolution representation (meta-cells) and smoothed via regression on averaged spliced expression before trajectory graph construction.
